    android-14.0.0_r2 to android-14.0.0_r3 AOSP changelog

    This only includes the Android Open Source Project changes and does not include any changes in any proprietary components included by Google or any hardware manufacturer. The raw log was generated using a modified version of this script written by JBQ and improved by Al Sutton.

    Please do not copy this without attribution to this site and JBQ for the original script.

    +- Project: platform/art

    f2bbf6d3c7 : Fix a bug in image generation due to default conflict methods. 5f6e896433 : Expand visibility to include Go modules d955ee5d83 : Don't use timestamp counters on ARM32 debd82f9b1 : art: disable standalone cts tests for native-bridge f44ad0e8fc : Remove RootTargetPreparer from art_standalone_artd_tests. 19e0ef6c9f : Do not preload java.util.ImmutableCollections.

    +- Project: platform/bionic

    78973da8e : Revert "Use proprety_socket_for_system if permissions allow" d1076eb72 : Revert "Fix deadlock caused by two-threaded property controls"
